Note.-Boats arrivin from full inward dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if discharging near the West Atation; but table they are all entered as local traders. In the same way boats from Shasi town to Tangyang pay full o r ues, but are classed among local elearances. Boats arrivmng from Tangyang for other ports pay full outward dues. They ar entered in the Shipping table first as local entries, then as interport clearances. Boats arriving from othar ports for Tangyang have usually no dues to pay, but in the Shipping table they are entered, firstly, as interport entries and, secondly, as local clearances. 11.-1 MPORTS. 1*. NoTICE is hereby given that the Taku Bar Inner Leading Marks have been shifted. The front nad, mrbi ie s%pole Vait u&quot;e aetsuamovted ig a black triangular shape, on which a dioptric, seventh order, fixed white light is exhibited from sunset to sunrise, is situated I.26 miles S. toj. W. of the Cavalier at North Fort. The rear mark, which is a pole painted white and surmounted by a black triangular shape, on which a dioptric, sixth order, fixed red light is exhibited from sunset to sunrise, is situated 2,162 feet N. 720 15&#39; W. from the front mark. These marks in line lead through the channel between the Bar Light-boat and the Spit Light-boat. All bearings given are magnetic. SHANGHAI, 24th JTu/ 1913.

290 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [juLy-sRPT., 1913.1 No. 558. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. NORTHERN APPROACH TO THE YANGTZE RIVER. SHAWEISHAN LIGHT-STATION-FoG SIREN ESTABLISHED. NOTICE is hereby given that a first order fog siren has been established at Shaweishan Light-station. The siren, which, on and after the 25th September 1913, will be sounded during foggy or thick weather, is elevated 190 feet above sea level, and will give three blasts of equal pitch in quick succession every i4 minutes, thus:- Blast...... ............. 2 secoids. Silence ..... .......... 3 Blast . . . . . . . . . . . . . 2 Silence..... .......... 3 Blast.. ............. . 24 Silence . . . . . . . . . . 76 , To the southward the intensity of the sound of the siren is lessened owing to it being obstructed by the high part of the island.
